```
   118→
   119→def _session_payload_or_exit(session_id: str) -> tuple[Path, dict[str, Any]]:
   120→    _validate_session_id_or_exit(session_id)
   121→    path = _session_file(session_id)
   122→    payload = _load_json_object(path, label="session")
   123→    payload_id = str(payload.get("session_id", "")).strip()
   124→    if payload_id != session_id:
   125→        print(
   126→            colorize(
   127→                f"  Error: session id mismatch in {path} (expected {session_id}, found {payload_id or '<missing>'}).",
   128→                "red",
   129→            ),
   130→            file=sys.stderr,
   131→        )
   132→        sys.exit(1)
   133→    return path, payload
   134→
   135→
   136→def _prepare_packet_snapshot(
   137→    args,
   138→    state: dict,
   139→    lang,
   140→    *,
   141→    config: dict[str, Any],
   142→) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], Path, Path]:
   143→    """Prepare holistic review packet and persist immutable+blind snapshots."""
   144→    path = Path(getattr(args, "path", ".") or ".")
   145→    dims = parse_dimensions(args)
   146→    dimensions = list(dims) if dims else None
   147→    retrospective = bool(getattr(args, "retrospective", False))
   148→    retrospective_max_issues = coerce_positive_int(
   149→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_issues", None),
   150→        default=30,
   151→    )
   152→    retrospective_max_batch_items = coerce_positive_int(
   153→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_batch_items", None),
   154→        default=20,
   155→    )
   156→    lang_run, found_files = setup_lang_concrete(lang, path, config)
   157→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   158→        state,
   159→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_run.name, command="review"),
   160→    )
   161→    packet = review_mod.prepare_holistic_review(
   162→        path,
   163→        lang_run,
   164→        state,
   165→        options=review_mod.HolisticReviewPrepareOptions(
   166→            dimensions=dimensions,
   167→            files=found_files or None,
   168→            include_issue_history=retrospective,
   169→            issue_history_max_issues=retrospective_max_issues,
   170→            issue_history_max_batch_items=retrospective_max_batch_items,
   171→        ),
   172→    )
   173→    packet["narrative"] = narrative
   174→    next_command = "desloppify review --external-submit --session-id <id> --import <file>"
   175→    if retrospective:
   176→        next_command += (
   177→            " --retrospective"
   178→            f" --retrospective-max-issues {retrospective_max_issues}"
   179→            f" --retrospective-max-batch-items {retrospective_max_batch_items}"
   180→        )
   181→    packet["next_command"] = next_command
   182→    write_query(packet)
   183→
   184→    stamp = runner_helpers_mod.run_stamp()
   185→    blind_packet_path = _blind_packet_path()
   186→    packet_path, blind_path = runner_helpers_mod.write_packet_snapshot(
   187→        packet,
   188→        stamp=stamp,
   189→        review_packet_dir=_review_packet_dir(),
   190→        blind_path=blind_packet_path,
   191→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   192→    )
   193→    return packet, packet_path, blind_path
   194→
   195→
   196→def _build_template_payload(packet: dict[str, Any], *, session_id: str, token: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
   197→    dimensions = [
   198→        dim
   199→        for dim in packet.get("dimensions", [])
   200→        if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()
   201→    ]
   202→    return {
   203→        "session": {
   204→            "id": session_id,
   205→            "token": token,
   206→        },
   207→        "assessments": {dim: 0 for dim in dimensions},
   208→        "findings": [],
   209→    }

   212→def _build_claude_launch_prompt(
   213→    *,
   214→    session_id: str,
   215→    token: str,
   216→    blind_path: Path,
   217→    template_path: Path,
   218→    output_path: Path,
   219→) -> str:
   220→    """Build a copy/paste-ready prompt for a Claude blind reviewer subagent."""
   221→    return "\n".join(
   222→        [
   223→            "# Claude Blind Reviewer Launch Prompt",
   224→            "",
   225→            "You are an isolated blind reviewer. Do not use prior chat context, prior score history, or target-score anchoring.",
   226→            "",
   227→            f"Blind packet: {blind_path}",

   238→            "7. Do not include provenance metadata (CLI injects canonical provenance).",
   239→            "8. Return JSON only (no markdown fences).",

   244→def do_external_start(args, state, lang, *, config: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> None:
   245→    """Start an external review session with CLI-issued provenance context."""
   246→    config = config or {}

   278→    session_id = _session_id()

   373→
   374→def _canonical_external_payload(
   375→    raw_payload: dict[str, Any],
   376→    *,
   377→    session: dict[str, Any],
   378→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   379→    """Return import payload with canonical provenance and required session token."""
   380→    session_meta = raw_payload.get("session")
   381→    if not isinstance(session_meta, dict):
   382→        print(
   383→            colorize(
   384→                "  Error: external reviewer payload must include top-level `session` object.",
   385→                "red",
   386→            ),
   387→            file=sys.stderr,
   388→        )
   389→        print(
   390→            colorize(
   391→                "  Expected: {\"session\":{\"id\":\"...\",\"token\":\"...\"},\"assessments\":{...},\"findings\":[...]}",
   392→                "dim",
   393→            ),
   394→            file=sys.stderr,
   395→        )
   396→        sys.exit(1)
   397→
   398→    payload_id = str(session_meta.get("id", "")).strip()
   399→    payload_token = str(session_meta.get("token", "")).strip()
   400→    expected_id = str(session.get("session_id", "")).strip()
   401→    expected_token = str(session.get("token", "")).strip()
   402→    if payload_id != expected_id or payload_token != expected_token:
   403→        print(
   404→            colorize(
   405→                "  Error: session id/token mismatch in external reviewer payload.",
   406→                "red",
   407→            ),
   408→            file=sys.stderr,
   409→        )
   410→        print(
   411→            colorize("  Regenerate output using the session template/instructions.", "yellow"),
   412→            file=sys.stderr,
   413→        )
   414→        sys.exit(1)
   415→
   416→    payload = dict(raw_payload)
   417→    payload.pop("session", None)
   418→    payload.pop("provenance", None)
   419→    payload["provenance"] = {
   420→        "kind": "blind_review_batch_import",
   421→        "blind": True,
   422→        "runner": str(session.get("runner", "claude")),
   423→        "run_stamp": str(session.get("session_id", "")),
   424→        "created_at": _iso_seconds(_utc_now()),
   425→        "packet_path": str(session.get("blind_packet_path", "")),
   426→        "packet_sha256": str(session.get("packet_sha256", "")),
   427→        "external_session_id": expected_id,
   428→    }
   429→    return payload
   430→
   431→
   432→def _ensure_session_open_or_exit(session: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   433→    status = str(session.get("status", "open")).strip().lower()
   434→    if status == "open":
   435→        return
   436→    print(
   437→        colorize(
   438→            f"  Error: session is not open (status={status or 'unknown'}). Start a new session with --external-start.",
   439→            "red",
   440→        ),
   441→        file=sys.stderr,
   442→    )
   443→    sys.exit(1)
   444→
   445→
   446→def _ensure_session_not_expired_or_exit(session: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   447→    expires_at = _parse_iso(session.get("expires_at"))
   448→    if expires_at is None:
   449→        print(
   450→            colorize(
   451→                "  Error: session metadata is missing/invalid expires_at.",
   452→                "red",
   453→            ),
   454→            file=sys.stderr,
   455→        )
   456→        sys.exit(1)
   457→    now = _utc_now()
   458→    if now <= expires_at:
   459→        return
   460→    print(
   461→        colorize(
   462→            f"  Error: session expired at {session.get('expires_at')}. Start a new session with --external-start.",
   463→            "red",
   464→        ),
   465→        file=sys.stderr,
   466→    )
   467→    sys.exit(1)
   468→
   469→
   470→def do_external_submit(
   471→    *,
   472→    import_file: str,
   473→    session_id: str,
   474→    state: dict,
   475→    lang,
   476→    state_file,
   477→    config: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   478→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   479→    scan_after_import: bool = False,
   480→    scan_path: str = ".",
   481→    dry_run: bool = False,
   482→) -> None:
   483→    """Submit external reviewer output via session, adding canonical provenance."""
   484→    config = config or {}
   485→    session_path, session = _session_payload_or_exit(session_id)
   486→    _ensure_session_open_or_exit(session)
   487→    _ensure_session_not_expired_or_exit(session)
   488→
   489→    if str(session.get("runner", "")).strip().lower() not in _EXTERNAL_SUPPORTED_RUNNERS:
   490→        print(
   491→            colorize(
   492→                "  Error: only Claude external sessions currently support durable score submit.",
   493→                "red",
   494→            ),
   495→            file=sys.stderr,
   496→        )
   497→        sys.exit(1)
   498→
   499→    findings_path = Path(import_file)
   500→    raw_payload = _load_json_object(findings_path, label="external findings")
   501→    canonical_payload = _canonical_external_payload(raw_payload, session=session)
   502→
   503→    stamp = runner_helpers_mod.run_stamp()
   504→    session_dir = session_path.parent
   505→    canonical_path = session_dir / f"canonical_import_{stamp}.json"
   506→    safe_write_text(canonical_path, json.dumps(canonical_payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   507→
   508→    if dry_run:
   509→        do_validate_import(
   510→            str(canonical_path),
   511→            lang,
   512→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   513→            attested_external=True,
   514→            manual_attest=str(session.get("attest", EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT)),
   515→        )
   516→        return
   517→
   518→    do_import(
   519→        str(canonical_path),
   520→        state,
   521→        lang,
   522→        state_file,
   523→        config=config,
   524→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   525→        attested_external=True,
   526→        manual_attest=str(session.get("attest", EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT)),
   527→    )
   528→
   529→    submitted_at = _iso_seconds(_utc_now())
   530→    session["status"] = "submitted"
   531→    session["submitted_at"] = submitted_at
   532→    session["submitted_input_file"] = str(findings_path)
   533→    session["submitted_canonical_file"] = str(canonical_path)
   534→    safe_write_text(session_path, json.dumps(session, indent=2) + "\n")
   535→
   536→    if scan_after_import:
   537→        from .batch import FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS
   538→
   539→        code = runner_helpers_mod.run_followup_scan(
   540→            lang_name=lang.name,
   541→            scan_path=scan_path,
   542→            deps=runner_helpers_mod.FollowupScanDeps(
   543→                project_root=_runtime_project_root(),
   544→                timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   545→                python_executable=sys.executable,
   546→                subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   547→                timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   548→                colorize_fn=colorize,
   549→            ),
   550→        )
   551→        if code != 0:
   552→            raise SystemExit(code)
   553→
   554→
   555→__all__ = ["do_external_start", "do_external_submit", "EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT"]
   556→
```

```
   105→        abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   106→        abstraction_component_names=ABSTRACTION_COMPONENT_NAMES,
   107→    )
   108→
   109→
   110→def _load_or_prepare_packet(
   111→    args,
   112→    *,
   113→    state: dict,
   114→    lang,
   115→    config: dict,
   116→    stamp: str,
   117→) -> tuple[dict, Path, Path]:
   118→    """Load packet override or prepare a fresh packet snapshot."""
   119→    packet_override = getattr(args, "packet", None)
   120→    if packet_override:
   121→        packet_path = Path(packet_override)
   122→        if not packet_path.exists():
   123→            print_error(f"packet not found: {packet_override}")
   124→            sys.exit(1)
   125→        try:
   126→            packet = json.loads(packet_path.read_text())
   127→        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   128→            print_error(f"reading packet: {exc}")
   129→            sys.exit(1)
   130→        blind_path = _blind_packet_path()
   131→        blind_packet = runner_helpers_mod.build_blind_packet(packet)
   132→        safe_write_text(blind_path, json.dumps(blind_packet, indent=2) + "\n")
   133→        print(colorize(f"  Immutable packet: {packet_path}", "dim"))
   134→        print(colorize(f"  Blind packet: {blind_path}", "dim"))
   135→        return packet, packet_path, blind_path
   136→
   137→    path = Path(args.path)
   138→    dims = parse_dimensions(args)
   139→    dimensions = list(dims) if dims else None
   140→    retrospective = bool(getattr(args, "retrospective", False))
   141→    retrospective_max_issues = coerce_positive_int(
   142→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_issues", None),
   143→        default=30,
   144→        minimum=1,
   145→    )
   146→    retrospective_max_batch_items = coerce_positive_int(
   147→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_batch_items", None),
   148→        default=20,
   149→        minimum=1,
   150→    )
   151→    lang_run, found_files = _setup_lang(lang, path, config)
   152→    lang_name = lang_run.name
   153→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   154→        state,
   155→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="review"),
   156→    )
   157→
   158→    blind_path = _blind_packet_path()
   159→    packet = review_mod.prepare_holistic_review(
   160→        path,
   161→        lang_run,
   162→        state,
   163→        options=review_mod.HolisticReviewPrepareOptions(
   164→            dimensions=dimensions,
   165→            files=found_files or None,
   166→            max_files_per_batch=_coerce_review_batch_file_limit(config),
   167→            include_issue_history=retrospective,
   168→            issue_history_max_issues=retrospective_max_issues,
   169→            issue_history_max_batch_items=retrospective_max_batch_items,
   170→        ),
   171→    )
   172→    packet["config"] = _redacted_review_config(config)
   173→    packet["narrative"] = narrative
   174→    next_command = "desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel"
   175→    if retrospective:
   176→        next_command += (
   177→            " --retrospective"
   178→            f" --retrospective-max-issues {retrospective_max_issues}"
   179→            f" --retrospective-max-batch-items {retrospective_max_batch_items}"
   180→        )
   181→    packet["next_command"] = next_command
   182→    write_query_best_effort(
   183→        packet,
   184→        context="review packet query update",
   185→    )
   186→    packet_path, blind_saved = runner_helpers_mod.write_packet_snapshot(
   187→        packet,
   188→        stamp=stamp,
   189→        review_packet_dir=_review_packet_dir(),
   190→        blind_path=blind_path,
   191→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   192→    )
   193→    print(colorize(f"  Immutable packet: {packet_path}", "dim"))
   194→    print(colorize(f"  Blind packet: {blind_saved}", "dim"))
   195→    return packet, packet_path, blind_saved
   196→
   197→
   198→def _do_run_batches(args, state, lang, state_file, config: dict | None = None) -> None:
   199→    """Run holistic investigation batches with a local subagent runner."""
   200→    runtime_project_root = _runtime_project_root()
   201→    batch_timeout_seconds = coerce_positive_int(
   202→        getattr(args, "batch_timeout_seconds", None),
   203→        default=CODEX_BATCH_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   204→        minimum=1,
   205→    )
   206→    batch_max_retries = coerce_positive_int(
   207→        getattr(args, "batch_max_retries", None),
   208→        default=1,
   209→        minimum=0,
   210→    )
   211→    batch_retry_backoff_seconds = coerce_non_negative_float(
   212→        getattr(args, "batch_retry_backoff_seconds", None),
   213→        default=2.0,
   214→    )
   215→    batch_heartbeat_seconds = coerce_non_negative_float(
   216→        getattr(args, "batch_heartbeat_seconds", None),
   217→        default=15.0,
   218→    )
   219→    batch_live_log_interval_seconds = (
   220→        max(1.0, min(batch_heartbeat_seconds, 10.0))
   221→        if batch_heartbeat_seconds > 0
   222→        else 5.0
   223→    )
   224→    batch_stall_kill_seconds = coerce_non_negative_int(
   225→        getattr(args, "batch_stall_kill_seconds", None),
   226→        default=CODEX_BATCH_STALL_KILL_SECONDS,
   227→    )
   228→
   229→    def _prepare_run_artifacts(*, stamp, selected_indexes, batches, packet_path, run_root, repo_root):
   230→        return runner_helpers_mod.prepare_run_artifacts(
   231→            stamp=stamp,
   232→            selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   233→            batches=batches,
   234→            packet_path=packet_path,
   235→            run_root=run_root,
   236→            repo_root=repo_root,
   237→            build_prompt_fn=batch_core_mod.build_batch_prompt,
   238→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   239→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   240→        )
   241→
   242→    def _collect_batch_results(*, selected_indexes, failures, output_files, allowed_dims):
   243→        return runner_helpers_mod.collect_batch_results(
   244→            selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   245→            failures=failures,
   246→            output_files=output_files,
   247→            allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   248→            extract_payload_fn=lambda raw: batch_core_mod.extract_json_payload(raw, log_fn=log),
   249→            normalize_result_fn=lambda payload, dims: batch_core_mod.normalize_batch_result(
   250→                payload,
   251→                dims,
   252→                max_batch_findings=max_batch_findings_for_dimension_count(
   253→                    len(dims)
   254→                ),
   255→                abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   256→            ),
   257→        )
   258→
   259→    return review_batches_mod.do_run_batches(
   260→        args,
   261→        state,
   262→        lang,
   263→        state_file,
   264→        config=config,
   265→        run_stamp_fn=runner_helpers_mod.run_stamp,
   266→        load_or_prepare_packet_fn=_load_or_prepare_packet,
   267→        selected_batch_indexes_fn=lambda args, *, batch_count: runner_helpers_mod.selected_batch_indexes(
   268→            raw_selection=getattr(args, "only_batches", None),
   269→            batch_count=batch_count,
   270→            parse_fn=batch_core_mod.parse_batch_selection,
   271→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   272→        ),
   273→        prepare_run_artifacts_fn=_prepare_run_artifacts,
   274→        run_codex_batch_fn=lambda *, prompt, repo_root, output_file, log_file: runner_helpers_mod.run_codex_batch(
   275→            prompt=prompt,
   276→            repo_root=repo_root,
   277→            output_file=output_file,
   278→            log_file=log_file,
   279→            deps=runner_helpers_mod.CodexBatchRunnerDeps(
   280→                timeout_seconds=batch_timeout_seconds,
   281→                subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   282→                timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   283→                safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   284→                use_popen_runner=(getattr(subprocess.run, "__module__", "") == "subprocess"),
   285→                subprocess_popen=subprocess.Popen,
   286→                live_log_interval_seconds=batch_live_log_interval_seconds,
   287→                stall_after_output_seconds=batch_stall_kill_seconds,
   288→                max_retries=batch_max_retries,
   289→                retry_backoff_seconds=batch_retry_backoff_seconds,
   290→            ),
   291→        ),
   292→        execute_batches_fn=runner_helpers_mod.execute_batches,
   293→        collect_batch_results_fn=_collect_batch_results,
   294→        print_failures_fn=runner_helpers_mod.print_failures,
   295→        print_failures_and_exit_fn=runner_helpers_mod.print_failures_and_exit,
   296→        merge_batch_results_fn=_merge_batch_results,
   297→        build_import_provenance_fn=runner_helpers_mod.build_batch_import_provenance,
   298→        do_import_fn=_do_import,
   299→        run_followup_scan_fn=lambda *, lang_name, scan_path: runner_helpers_mod.run_followup_scan(
   300→            lang_name=lang_name,
   301→            scan_path=scan_path,
   302→            deps=runner_helpers_mod.FollowupScanDeps(

   305→                python_executable=sys.executable,
```

```
   100→def _collect_reviewed_files_from_batches(
   101→    *,
   102→    batches: list[dict[str, object]],
   103→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   104→) -> list[str]:
   105→    """Collect normalized file paths reviewed in the selected batch set."""
   106→    reviewed: list[str] = []
   107→    seen: set[str] = set()
   108→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   109→        if idx < 0 or idx >= len(batches):
   110→            continue
   111→        batch = batches[idx]
   112→        files = batch.get("files_to_read", [])
   113→        if not isinstance(files, list):
   114→            continue
   115→        for raw in files:
   116→            if not isinstance(raw, str):
   117→                continue
   118→            path = raw.strip().strip(",'\"")
   119→            if not path or path in {".", ".."}:
   120→                continue
   121→            if path.endswith("/"):
   122→                continue
   123→            if path in seen:
   124→                continue
   125→            seen.add(path)
   126→            reviewed.append(path)
   127→    return reviewed
   128→
   129→
   130→def _coerce_positive_int(value: object, *, default: int, minimum: int = 1) -> int:
   131→    """Parse positive integer CLI/config inputs with a safe default."""
   132→    if value is None:
   133→        return default
   134→    if not isinstance(value, int | float | str):
   135→        return default
   136→    try:
   137→        parsed = int(value)
   138→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   139→        return default
   140→    return parsed if parsed >= minimum else default
   141→
   142→
   143→def _coerce_positive_float(value: object, *, default: float, minimum: float = 0.1) -> float:
   144→    """Parse positive float CLI/config inputs with a safe default."""
   145→    if value is None:
   146→        return default
   147→    if not isinstance(value, int | float | str):
   148→        return default
   149→    try:
   150→        parsed = float(value)
   151→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   152→        return default
   153→    return parsed if parsed >= minimum else default
   154→
   155→
   156→def _coerce_non_negative_float(value: object, *, default: float) -> float:
   157→    """Parse non-negative float CLI/config inputs with a safe default."""
   158→    if value is None:
   159→        return default
   160→    if not isinstance(value, int | float | str):
   161→        return default
   162→    try:
   163→        parsed = float(value)
   164→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   165→        return default
   166→    return parsed if parsed >= 0.0 else default
   167→
   168→
   169→def _normalize_dimension_list(raw: object) -> list[str]:
   170→    """Normalize dimension collections to a stable, de-duplicated list."""
   171→    if not isinstance(raw, list):
   172→        return []
   173→    out: list[str] = []
   174→    seen: set[str] = set()
   175→    for item in raw:
   176→        if not isinstance(item, str):
   177→            continue
   178→        dim = item.strip()
   179→        if not dim or dim in seen:
   180→            continue
   181→        seen.add(dim)
   182→        out.append(dim)
   183→    return out
   184→

   186→def _scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name: str) -> list[str]:
   187→    """Return default scored subjective dimensions for one language."""
   188→    try:
   189→        default_dims, _, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   190→    except (ValueError, RuntimeError):
   191→        return []
   192→    return _normalize_dimension_list(default_dims)

   195→def _missing_scored_dimensions(
   196→    *,
   197→    selected_dims: list[str],
   198→    scored_dims: list[str],

   204→def _missing_dimensions_command(*, missing_dims: list[str], scan_path: str) -> str:
   205→    """Return rerun command for missing subjective dimensions."""
   206→    base = "desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import"
   207→    if scan_path and scan_path != ".":
   208→        base += f" --path {shlex.quote(scan_path)}"
   209→    if missing_dims:
   210→        base += f" --dimensions {','.join(missing_dims)}"

   299→def do_run_batches(
   300→    args,
   301→    state,
   302→    lang,
   303→    state_file,
   304→    *,
   305→    config: dict | None,
   306→    run_stamp_fn,

   321→    project_root: Path,

   323→) -> None:
   324→    """Run holistic investigation batches with a local subagent runner."""
   325→    config = config or {}

   327→    _validate_runner(runner, colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   328→    allow_partial = bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False))

   330→    max_parallel_batches = _coerce_positive_int(

   340→    batch_timeout_seconds = _coerce_positive_int(

   342→        default=2 * 60 * 60,
   343→        minimum=1,
   344→    )
   345→    batch_max_retries = _coerce_positive_int(
   346→        getattr(args, "batch_max_retries", None),
   347→        default=1,
   348→        minimum=0,
   349→    )
   350→    batch_retry_backoff_seconds = _coerce_non_negative_float(
   351→        getattr(args, "batch_retry_backoff_seconds", None),
   352→        default=2.0,
   353→    )
   354→    raw_stall_warning = getattr(args, "batch_stall_warning_seconds", None)
   355→    stall_warning_seconds = 0
   356→    if isinstance(raw_stall_warning, int | float | str):
   357→        try:
   358→            parsed_stall_warning = int(raw_stall_warning)
   359→        except (TypeError, ValueError):
   360→            parsed_stall_warning = 0
   361→        stall_warning_seconds = parsed_stall_warning if parsed_stall_warning > 0 else 0
   362→    raw_stall_kill = getattr(args, "batch_stall_kill_seconds", None)
   363→    stall_kill_seconds = 0
   364→    if isinstance(raw_stall_kill, int | float | str):
   365→        try:
   366→            parsed_stall_kill = int(raw_stall_kill)
   367→        except (TypeError, ValueError):
   368→            parsed_stall_kill = 0
   369→        stall_kill_seconds = parsed_stall_kill if parsed_stall_kill > 0 else 0
   370→
   371→    stamp = run_stamp_fn()
   372→    packet, immutable_packet_path, prompt_packet_path = load_or_prepare_packet_fn(
   373→        args,
   374→        state=state,
   375→        lang=lang,
   376→        config=config,
   377→        stamp=stamp,
   378→    )
   379→
   380→    scan_path = str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or ".")
   381→    packet_dimensions = _normalize_dimension_list(packet.get("dimensions", []))
   382→    scored_dimensions = _scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang.name)
   383→    _print_preflight_dimension_scope_notice(
   384→        selected_dims=packet_dimensions,
   385→        scored_dims=scored_dimensions,
   386→        explicit_selection=bool(getattr(args, "dimensions", None)),
   387→        scan_path=scan_path,
   388→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   389→    )
   390→    suggested_prepare_cmd = f"desloppify review --prepare --path {scan_path}"
   391→    batches = _require_batches(
   392→        packet,
   393→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   394→        suggested_prepare_cmd=suggested_prepare_cmd,
   395→    )
   396→
   397→    selected_indexes = selected_batch_indexes_fn(args, batch_count=len(batches))
   398→    total_batches = len(selected_indexes)
   399→    effective_workers = min(total_batches, max_parallel_batches) if run_parallel else 1
   400→    waves = max(1, math.ceil(total_batches / max(1, effective_workers)))
   401→    worst_case_seconds = waves * batch_timeout_seconds
   402→    worst_case_minutes = max(1, math.ceil(worst_case_seconds / 60))
   403→    print(
   404→        colorize_fn(
   405→            "  Runtime expectation: "
   406→            f"{total_batches} batch(es), workers={effective_workers}, "
   407→            f"timeout-per-batch={int(batch_timeout_seconds / 60)}m, "
   408→            f"worst-case upper bound ~{worst_case_minutes}m.",
   409→            "dim",
   410→        )
   411→    )
   412→    run_dir, logs_dir, prompt_files, output_files, log_files = prepare_run_artifacts_fn(
   413→        stamp=stamp,
   414→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   415→        batches=batches,
   416→        packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   417→        run_root=subagent_runs_dir,
   418→        repo_root=project_root,
   419→    )
   420→    raw_run_log_file = getattr(args, "run_log_file", None)
   421→    run_log_file_value = (
   422→        raw_run_log_file.strip()
   423→        if isinstance(raw_run_log_file, str) and raw_run_log_file.strip()
   424→        else None
   425→    )
   426→    if run_log_file_value is not None:
   427→        candidate = Path(run_log_file_value).expanduser()
   428→        run_log_path = candidate if candidate.is_absolute() else project_root / candidate
   429→    else:
   430→        run_log_path = run_dir / "run.log"
   431→    run_log_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   432→
   433→    def _append_run_log(message: str) -> None:
   434→        line = f"{datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec='seconds')} {message}\n"
   435→        try:
   436→            with run_log_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
   437→                handle.write(line)
   438→        except OSError:
   439→            return
   440→
   441→    _append_run_log(
   442→        "run-start "
   443→        f"runner={runner} parallel={run_parallel} max_parallel={max_parallel_batches} "
   444→        f"timeout={batch_timeout_seconds}s heartbeat={heartbeat_seconds:.1f}s "
   445→        f"stall_warning={stall_warning_seconds}s stall_kill={stall_kill_seconds}s "
   446→        f"retries={batch_max_retries} "
   447→        f"retry_backoff={batch_retry_backoff_seconds:.1f}s upper_bound={worst_case_minutes}m "
   448→        f"selected={[idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes]}"
   449→    )
   450→    _append_run_log(f"run-path {run_dir}")
   451→    _append_run_log(f"packet {immutable_packet_path}")
   452→    _append_run_log(f"blind-packet {prompt_packet_path}")
   453→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Live run log: {run_log_path}", "dim"))
   454→
   455→    if getattr(args, "dry_run", False):
   456→        print(
   457→            colorize_fn(
   458→                "  Dry run only: prompts generated, runner execution skipped.", "yellow"
   459→            )
   460→        )
   461→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Immutable packet: {immutable_packet_path}", "dim"))
   462→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Blind packet: {prompt_packet_path}", "dim"))
   463→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Prompts: {run_dir / 'prompts'}", "dim"))
   464→        _append_run_log("run-finished dry-run")
   465→        return
   466→
   467→    def _run_batch(*, prompt: str, output_file: Path, log_file: Path) -> int:
   468→        return run_codex_batch_fn(
   469→            prompt=prompt,
   470→            repo_root=project_root,
   471→            output_file=output_file,
   472→            log_file=log_file,
   473→        )
   474→
   475→    def _make_task(batch_index: int):
   476→        prompt_path = prompt_files[batch_index]
   477→        output_path = output_files[batch_index]
   478→        log_path = log_files[batch_index]
   479→
   480→        def _task() -> int:
   481→            try:
   482→                prompt = prompt_path.read_text()
   483→            except OSError as exc:
   484→                raise RuntimeError(
   485→                    f"unable to read prompt for batch #{batch_index + 1}: {prompt_path}"
   486→                ) from exc
   487→            return _run_batch(prompt=prompt, output_file=output_path, log_file=log_path)
   488→
   489→        return _task
   490→
   491→    tasks = {idx: _make_task(idx) for idx in selected_indexes}
   492→
   493→    batch_positions = {batch_idx: pos + 1 for pos, batch_idx in enumerate(selected_indexes)}
   494→    summary_created_at = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   495→    stall_warned_batches: set[int] = set()
   496→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {
   497→        str(idx + 1): {
   498→            "position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0),
   499→            "status": "pending",
   500→            "prompt_path": str(prompt_files[idx]),
   501→            "result_path": str(output_files[idx]),
   502→            "log_path": str(log_files[idx]),
   503→        }
   504→        for idx in selected_indexes
   505→    }
   506→
   507→    if run_parallel:
   508→        print(
   509→            colorize_fn(
   510→                "  Parallel runner config: "
   511→                f"max-workers={min(total_batches, max_parallel_batches)}, "
   512→                f"heartbeat={heartbeat_seconds:.1f}s",
   513→                "dim",
   514→            )
   515→        )
   516→
   517→    def _report_progress(
   518→        batch_index: int,
   519→        event: str,
   520→        code: int | None = None,
   521→        **details,
   522→    ) -> None:
   523→        if event == "heartbeat":
   524→            active = details.get("active_batches")
   525→            queued = details.get("queued_batches", [])
   526→            elapsed = details.get("elapsed_seconds", {})
   527→            if not isinstance(active, list):
   528→                active = []
   529→            if not isinstance(queued, list):
   530→                queued = []
   531→            if not active and not queued:
   532→                return
   533→            segments: list[str] = []
   534→            for idx in active[:6]:
   535→                secs = 0
   536→                if isinstance(elapsed, dict):
   537→                    raw_secs = elapsed.get(idx, 0)
   538→                    secs = int(raw_secs) if isinstance(raw_secs, int | float) else 0
   539→                segments.append(f"#{idx + 1}:{secs}s")
   540→            if len(active) > 6:
   541→                segments.append(f"+{len(active) - 6} more")
   542→            queued_segment = ""
   543→            if queued:
   544→                queued_segment = f", queued {len(queued)}"
   545→            print(
   546→                colorize_fn(
   547→                    "  Batch heartbeat: "
   548→                    f"{len(active)}/{total_batches} active{queued_segment} "
   549→                    f"({', '.join(segments) if segments else 'running batches pending'})",
   550→                    "dim",
   551→                )
   552→            )
   553→            _append_run_log(
   554→                "heartbeat "
   555→                f"active={[idx + 1 for idx in active]} queued={[idx + 1 for idx in queued]} "
   556→                f"elapsed={{{', '.join(f'{idx + 1}:{elapsed.get(idx, 0)}' for idx in active)}}}"
   557→            )
   558→            if stall_warning_seconds > 0 and isinstance(elapsed, dict):
   559→                slow_active = [
   560→                    idx
   561→                    for idx in active
   562→                    if isinstance(elapsed.get(idx), int | float)
   563→                    and int(elapsed.get(idx) or 0) >= stall_warning_seconds
   564→                ]
   565→                newly_warned = [idx for idx in slow_active if idx not in stall_warned_batches]
   566→                if newly_warned:
   567→                    stall_warned_batches.update(newly_warned)
   568→                    warning_message = (
   569→                        "  Stall warning: batches "
   570→                        f"{[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]} exceeded "
   571→                        f"{stall_warning_seconds}s elapsed. "
   572→                        "This may be normal for long runs; review run.log and batch logs."
   573→                    )
   574→                    print(colorize_fn(warning_message, "yellow"))
   575→                    _append_run_log(
   576→                        "stall-warning "
   577→                        f"threshold={stall_warning_seconds}s batches={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]}"
   578→                    )
   579→            return
   580→
   581→        position = batch_positions.get(batch_index, 0)
   582→        key = str(batch_index + 1)
   583→        state = batch_status.setdefault(
   584→            key,
   585→            {
   586→                "position": position,
   587→                "status": "pending",
   588→                "prompt_path": str(prompt_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   589→                "result_path": str(output_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   590→                "log_path": str(log_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   591→            },
   592→        )
   593→        if event == "queued":
   594→            state["status"] = "queued"
   595→            print(
   596→                colorize_fn(
   597→                    f"  Batch {position}/{total_batches} queued (#{batch_index + 1})",
   598→                    "dim",
   599→                )
   600→            )
   601→            _append_run_log(f"batch-queued batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches}")
   602→            return
   603→        if event == "start":
   604→            state["status"] = "running"
   605→            state["started_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   606→            print(
   607→                colorize_fn(
   608→                    f"  Batch {position}/{total_batches} started (#{batch_index + 1})",
   609→                    "dim",
   610→                )
   611→            )
   612→            _append_run_log(f"batch-start batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches}")
   613→            return
   614→        if event == "done":
   615→            status = "done" if code == 0 else f"failed ({code})"
   616→            tone = "dim" if code == 0 else "yellow"
   617→            elapsed_seconds = details.get("elapsed_seconds")
   618→            elapsed_suffix = ""
   619→            if isinstance(elapsed_seconds, int | float):
   620→                elapsed_suffix = f" in {int(max(0, elapsed_seconds))}s"
   621→                state["elapsed_seconds"] = int(max(0, elapsed_seconds))
   622→            state["status"] = "succeeded" if code == 0 else "failed"
   623→            state["exit_code"] = int(code) if isinstance(code, int) else code
   624→            state["completed_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   625→            if batch_index in stall_warned_batches:
   626→                stall_warned_batches.discard(batch_index)
   627→            print(
   628→                colorize_fn(
   629→                    f"  Batch {position}/{total_batches} {status}{elapsed_suffix} (#{batch_index + 1})",
   630→                    tone,
   631→                )
   632→            )
   633→            _append_run_log(
   634→                f"batch-done batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches} "
   635→                f"code={code} elapsed={state.get('elapsed_seconds', 0)}"
   636→            )
   637→
   638→    def _record_execution_issue(batch_index: int, exc: Exception) -> None:
   639→        if batch_index < 0:
   640→            _append_run_log(f"execution-error heartbeat error={exc}")
   641→            return
   642→        _append_run_log(f"execution-error batch={batch_index + 1} error={exc}")
   643→
   644→    run_summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   645→
   646→    def _write_run_summary(
   647→        *,
   648→        successful_batches: list[int],
   649→        failed_batches: list[int],
   650→        interrupted: bool = False,
   651→        interruption_reason: str | None = None,
   652→    ) -> None:
   653→        run_summary: dict[str, object] = {
   654→            "created_at": summary_created_at,
   655→            "run_stamp": stamp,
   656→            "runner": runner,
   657→            "parallel": run_parallel,
   658→            "selected_batches": [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes],
   659→            "successful_batches": successful_batches,
   660→            "failed_batches": failed_batches,
   661→            "allow_partial": allow_partial,
   662→            "max_parallel_batches": max_parallel_batches if run_parallel else 1,
   663→            "batch_timeout_seconds": batch_timeout_seconds,
   664→            "batch_max_retries": batch_max_retries,
   665→            "batch_retry_backoff_seconds": batch_retry_backoff_seconds,
   666→            "batch_heartbeat_seconds": heartbeat_seconds if run_parallel else None,
   667→            "batch_stall_warning_seconds": stall_warning_seconds if run_parallel else None,
   668→            "batch_stall_kill_seconds": stall_kill_seconds,
   669→            "immutable_packet": str(immutable_packet_path),
   670→            "blind_packet": str(prompt_packet_path),
   671→            "run_dir": str(run_dir),
   672→            "logs_dir": str(logs_dir),
   673→            "run_log": str(run_log_path),
   674→            "batches": batch_status,
   675→        }
   676→        if interrupted:
   677→            run_summary["interrupted"] = True
   678→            if interruption_reason:
   679→                run_summary["interruption_reason"] = interruption_reason
   680→        safe_write_text_fn(run_summary_path, json.dumps(run_summary, indent=2) + "\n")
   681→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Run summary: {run_summary_path}", "dim"))
   682→        _append_run_log(f"run-summary {run_summary_path}")
   683→
   684→    try:
   685→        execution_failures = execute_batches_fn(
   686→            tasks=tasks,
   687→            run_parallel=run_parallel,
   688→            progress_fn=_report_progress,
   689→            error_log_fn=_record_execution_issue,
   690→            max_parallel_workers=max_parallel_batches,
   691→            heartbeat_seconds=heartbeat_seconds,
   692→        )
   693→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   694→        for idx in selected_indexes:
   695→            key = str(idx + 1)
   696→            state = batch_status.setdefault(
   697→                key,
   698→                {"position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   699→            )
   700→            if state.get("status") in {"pending", "queued", "running"}:
   701→                state["status"] = "interrupted"
   702→        _write_run_summary(
   703→            successful_batches=[],
   704→            failed_batches=[],
   705→            interrupted=True,
   706→            interruption_reason="keyboard_interrupt",
   707→        )
   708→        _append_run_log("run-interrupted reason=keyboard_interrupt")
   709→        raise SystemExit(130) from None
   710→
   711→    allowed_dims = {
   712→        str(dim) for dim in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(dim, str)
   713→    }
   714→    batch_results, failures = collect_batch_results_fn(
   715→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   716→        failures=execution_failures,
   717→        output_files=output_files,
   718→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   719→    )
   720→
   721→    execution_failure_set = set(execution_failures)
   722→    failure_set = set(failures)
   723→    successful_indexes = sorted(idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in failure_set)
   724→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   725→        key = str(idx + 1)
   726→        state = batch_status.setdefault(
   727→            key,
   728→            {"position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   729→        )
   730→        if idx not in failure_set:
   731→            state["status"] = "succeeded"
   732→            continue
   733→        if idx in execution_failure_set:
   734→            state["status"] = "failed"
   735→            continue
   736→        if not output_files[idx].exists():
   737→            state["status"] = "missing_output"
   738→            continue
   739→        state["status"] = "parse_failed"
   740→
   741→    _write_run_summary(
   742→        successful_batches=[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes],
   743→        failed_batches=[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)],
   744→    )
   745→
   746→    if failures and (not allow_partial or not batch_results):
   747→        _append_run_log(
   748→            f"run-finished failures={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]} mode=exit"
   749→        )
   750→        print_failures_and_exit_fn(
   751→            failures=failures,
   752→            packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   753→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   754→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   755→        )
   756→    elif failures:
   757→        print(
   758→            colorize_fn(
   759→                "  Partial completion enabled: importing successful batches and keeping failed batches open.",
   760→                "yellow",
   761→            )
   762→        )
   763→        print_failures_fn(
   764→            failures=failures,
   765→            packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   766→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   767→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   768→        )
   769→        _append_run_log(
   770→            "run-partial "
   771→            f"successful={[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes]} "
   772→            f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]}"
   773→        )
   774→
   775→    merged = merge_batch_results_fn(batch_results)
   776→    reviewed_files = _collect_reviewed_files_from_batches(
   777→        batches=batches,
   778→        selected_indexes=successful_indexes,
   779→    )
   780→    full_sweep_included = any(
   781→        str(batch.get("name", "")).strip().lower() == "full codebase sweep"
   782→        for idx in successful_indexes
   783→        if 0 <= idx < len(batches)
   784→        for batch in [batches[idx]]
   785→        if isinstance(batch, dict)
   786→    )
   787→    review_scope: dict[str, object] = {
   788→        "reviewed_files_count": len(reviewed_files),
   789→        "successful_batch_count": len(successful_indexes),
   790→        "full_sweep_included": full_sweep_included,
   791→    }
   792→    total_files = packet.get("total_files")
   793→    if isinstance(total_files, int) and not isinstance(total_files, bool) and total_files > 0:
   794→        review_scope["total_files"] = total_files
   795→    merged["review_scope"] = review_scope
   796→    if reviewed_files:
   797→        merged["reviewed_files"] = reviewed_files
   798→        print(
   799→            colorize_fn(
   800→                f"  Reviewed files captured for cache refresh: {len(reviewed_files)}",
   801→                "dim",
   802→            )
   803→        )
   804→    merged["provenance"] = build_import_provenance_fn(
   805→        runner=runner,
   806→        blind_packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   807→        run_stamp=stamp,
   808→        batch_indexes=successful_indexes,
   809→    )
   810→    merged_assessment_dims = _normalize_dimension_list(
   811→        list((merged.get("assessments") or {}).keys())
   812→    )
   813→    merged_finding_dims = _normalize_dimension_list(
   814→        [
   815→            finding.get("dimension")
   816→            for finding in (merged.get("findings") or [])
   817→            if isinstance(finding, dict)
   818→        ]
   819→    )
   820→    merged_imported_dims = _normalize_dimension_list(
   821→        merged_assessment_dims + merged_finding_dims
   822→    )
   823→    review_scope["imported_dimensions"] = merged_imported_dims
   824→    missing_after_import = _print_import_dimension_coverage_notice(
   825→        assessed_dims=merged_assessment_dims,
   826→        scored_dims=scored_dimensions,
   827→        scan_path=scan_path,
   828→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   829→    )
   830→    merged["assessment_coverage"] = {
   831→        "scored_dimensions": scored_dimensions,
   832→        "selected_dimensions": packet_dimensions,
   833→        "imported_dimensions": merged_assessment_dims,
   834→        "missing_dimensions": missing_after_import,
   835→    }
   836→    merged_path = run_dir / "holistic_findings_merged.json"
   837→    safe_write_text_fn(merged_path, json.dumps(merged, indent=2) + "\n")
   838→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Merged outputs: {merged_path}", "bold"))
   839→    _print_review_quality(merged.get("review_quality", {}), colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   840→
   841→    try:
   842→        do_import_fn(
   843→            str(merged_path),
   844→            state,
   845→            lang,
   846→            state_file,
   847→            config=config,
   848→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   849→            trusted_assessment_source=True,
   850→            trusted_assessment_label="trusted internal run-batches import",
   851→        )
   852→    except SystemExit as exc:
   853→        _append_run_log(f"run-finished import-failed code={exc.code}")
   854→        raise
   855→    except Exception as exc:
   856→        _append_run_log(f"run-finished import-error error={exc}")
   857→        raise
   858→    _append_run_log(
   859→        "run-finished "
   860→        f"successful={[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes]} "
   861→        f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]} imported={str(merged_path)}"
   862→    )
   863→
   864→    if getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False):
   865→        followup_code = run_followup_scan_fn(
   866→            lang_name=lang.name,
   867→            scan_path=str(args.path),
   868→        )
   869→        if followup_code != 0:
   870→            print(
   871→                colorize_fn(
   872→                    f"  Follow-up scan failed with exit code {followup_code}.",
   873→                    "red",
   874→                ),
   875→                file=sys.stderr,
   876→            )
   877→            raise SystemExit(followup_code)
   878→
   879→
   880→__all__ = ["do_run_batches"]
   881→
```

```
   173→def codex_batch_command(*, prompt: str, repo_root: Path, output_file: Path) -> list[str]:
   174→    """Build one codex exec command line for a batch prompt."""
   175→    effort = os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_CODEX_REASONING_EFFORT", "low").strip().lower()
   176→    if effort not in {"low", "medium", "high", "xhigh"}:
   177→        effort = "low"
   178→    return [
   179→        "codex",
   180→        "exec",
   181→        "--ephemeral",
   182→        "-C",
   183→        str(repo_root),
   184→        "-s",
   185→        "workspace-write",
   186→        "-c",
   187→        'approval_policy="never"',
   188→        "-c",
   189→        f'model_reasoning_effort="{effort}"',
   190→        "-o",
   191→        str(output_file),
   192→        prompt,
   193→    ]
   194→
   195→
   196→def _output_file_status_text(output_file: Path) -> str:
   197→    """Describe output file state for live log snapshots."""
   198→    if not output_file.exists():
   199→        return f"{output_file} (missing)"
   200→    try:
   201→        stat = output_file.stat()
   202→    except OSError as exc:
   203→        return f"{output_file} (exists; stat failed: {exc})"
   204→    modified_at = datetime.fromtimestamp(stat.st_mtime, tz=UTC).isoformat(
   205→        timespec="seconds"
   206→    )
   207→    return (
   208→        f"{output_file} (exists; bytes={stat.st_size}; "
   209→        f"modified={modified_at})"
   210→    )
   211→
   212→
   213→def _output_file_has_json_payload(output_file: Path) -> bool:
   214→    """Return True when the output file contains a valid JSON object."""
   215→    if not output_file.exists():
   216→        return False
   217→    try:
   218→        payload = json.loads(output_file.read_text())
   219→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
   220→        return False
   221→    return isinstance(payload, dict)
   222→
   223→
   224→def _terminate_process(process: subprocess.Popen[str]) -> None:
   225→    """Terminate (then kill) a subprocess that may still be running."""
   226→    if process.poll() is not None:
   227→        return
   228→    try:
   229→        process.terminate()
   230→        process.wait(timeout=3)
   231→        return
   232→    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
   233→        pass
   234→    try:
   235→        process.kill()
   236→        process.wait(timeout=3)
   237→    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
   238→        return
   239→
   240→
   241→def _drain_stream(stream, sink: list[str], state: _RunnerState) -> None:
   242→    """Read lines from *stream* into *sink*, updating activity timestamp."""
   243→    if stream is None:
   244→        return
   245→    try:
   246→        for chunk in iter(stream.readline, ""):
   247→            if not chunk:
   248→                break
   249→            with state.lock:
   250→                sink.append(chunk)
   251→                state.last_stream_activity = time.monotonic()
   252→    except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:  # pragma: no cover - defensive boundary
   253→        with state.lock:
   254→            sink.append(f"\n[stream read error: {exc}]\n")
   255→    finally:
   256→        try:
   257→            stream.close()
   258→        except (OSError, ValueError):
   259→            pass
   260→
   261→
   262→def _write_live_snapshot(state: _RunnerState, ctx: _AttemptContext) -> None:
   263→    """Write a point-in-time log snapshot while the runner is active."""
   264→    elapsed_seconds = int(max(0.0, time.monotonic() - ctx.started_monotonic))
   265→    with state.lock:
   266→        stdout_preview = "".join(state.stdout_chunks)
   267→        stderr_preview = "".join(state.stderr_chunks)
   268→        note = state.runner_note
   269→    note_block = f"\nRUNNER NOTE: {note}" if note else ""
   270→    ctx.safe_write_text_fn(
   271→        ctx.log_file,
   272→        "\n\n".join(
   273→            ctx.log_sections
   274→            + [
   275→                (
   276→                    f"{ctx.header}\n\n"
   277→                    "STATUS: running\n"
   278→                    f"STARTED AT: {ctx.started_at_iso}\n"
   279→                    f"ELAPSED: {elapsed_seconds}s\n"
   280→                    f"OUTPUT FILE: {_output_file_status_text(ctx.output_file)}"
   281→                    f"{note_block}\n\n"
   282→                    f"STDOUT (live):\n{stdout_preview}\n\n"
   283→                    f"STDERR (live):\n{stderr_preview}\n"
   284→                )
   285→            ]
   286→        ),
   287→    )
   288→
   289→
   290→def _start_live_writer(
   291→    state: _RunnerState, ctx: _AttemptContext, interval: float,
   292→) -> threading.Thread:
   293→    """Spawn a daemon thread that periodically writes live log snapshots."""
   294→    def _loop() -> None:
   295→        while not state.stop_event.wait(interval):
   296→            _write_live_snapshot(state, ctx)
   297→
   298→    thread = threading.Thread(target=_loop, daemon=True)
   299→    thread.start()
   300→    return thread
   301→
   302→
   303→def _check_stall(
   304→    output_file: Path,
   305→    prev_sig: tuple[int, int] | None,
   306→    prev_stable: float | None,
   307→    now: float,
   308→    last_activity: float,
   309→    threshold: int,
   310→) -> tuple[bool, tuple[int, int] | None, float | None]:
   311→    """Check for runner stall. Returns (stalled, new_sig, new_stable_since)."""
   312→    try:
   313→        stat = output_file.stat()
   314→        current_signature: tuple[int, int] | None = (int(stat.st_size), int(stat.st_mtime))
   315→    except OSError:
   316→        current_signature = None
   317→    if current_signature is None:
   318→        # If no output file ever appears, still recover from a silent hang once
   319→        # both output state and process streams have been idle long enough.
   320→        baseline = (
   321→            prev_stable if isinstance(prev_stable, int | float) else now
   322→        )
   323→        output_age = now - baseline
   324→        stream_idle = now - last_activity
   325→        if output_age >= threshold and stream_idle >= threshold:
   326→            return True, None, baseline
   327→        return False, None, baseline
   328→    if current_signature != prev_sig:
   329→        return False, current_signature, now
   330→    if prev_stable is None:
   331→        return False, prev_sig, prev_stable
   332→    output_age = now - prev_stable
   333→    stream_idle = now - last_activity
   334→    if output_age >= threshold and stream_idle >= threshold:
   335→        return True, prev_sig, prev_stable
   336→    return False, prev_sig, prev_stable
   337→
   338→
   339→def _run_via_popen(
   340→    cmd: list[str],
   341→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
   342→    state: _RunnerState,
   343→    ctx: _AttemptContext,
   344→    interval: float,
   345→    stall_seconds: int,
   346→) -> _ExecutionResult:
   347→    """Execute batch via Popen with live streaming and stall recovery."""
   348→    writer_thread = _start_live_writer(state, ctx, interval)
   349→    try:
   350→        process = deps.subprocess_popen(
   351→            cmd,
   352→            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
   353→            stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
   354→            text=True,
   355→            bufsize=1,
   356→        )
   357→    except OSError as exc:
   358→        state.stop_event.set()
   359→        writer_thread.join(timeout=2)
   360→        ctx.log_sections.append(f"{ctx.header}\n\nRUNNER ERROR:\n{exc}\n")
   361→        ctx.safe_write_text_fn(ctx.log_file, "\n\n".join(ctx.log_sections))
   362→        return _ExecutionResult(code=127, stdout_text="", stderr_text="", early_return=127)
   363→    except (
   364→        RuntimeError,
   365→        ValueError,
   366→        TypeError,
   367→        subprocess.SubprocessError,
   368→    ) as exc:  # pragma: no cover - defensive boundary
   369→        state.stop_event.set()
   370→        writer_thread.join(timeout=2)
   371→        ctx.log_sections.append(f"{ctx.header}\n\nUNEXPECTED RUNNER ERROR:\n{exc}\n")
   372→        ctx.safe_write_text_fn(ctx.log_file, "\n\n".join(ctx.log_sections))
   373→        return _ExecutionResult(code=1, stdout_text="", stderr_text="", early_return=1)
   374→
   375→    stdout_thread = threading.Thread(
   376→        target=_drain_stream,
   377→        args=(process.stdout, state.stdout_chunks, state),
   378→        daemon=True,
   379→    )
   380→    stderr_thread = threading.Thread(
   381→        target=_drain_stream,
   382→        args=(process.stderr, state.stderr_chunks, state),
   383→        daemon=True,
   384→    )
   385→    stdout_thread.start()
   386→    stderr_thread.start()
   387→
   388→    timed_out = False
   389→    stalled = False
   390→    recovered_from_stall = False
   391→    output_signature: tuple[int, int] | None = None
   392→    output_stable_since: float | None = None
   393→
   394→    while process.poll() is None:
   395→        now_monotonic = time.monotonic()
   396→        elapsed = int(max(0.0, now_monotonic - ctx.started_monotonic))
   397→        if elapsed >= deps.timeout_seconds:
   398→            with state.lock:
   399→                state.runner_note = f"timeout after {deps.timeout_seconds}s"
   400→            timed_out = True
   401→            _terminate_process(process)
   402→            break
   403→        if stall_seconds > 0:
   404→            with state.lock:
   405→                last_activity = state.last_stream_activity
   406→            stalled, output_signature, output_stable_since = _check_stall(
   407→                ctx.output_file,
   408→                output_signature,
   409→                output_stable_since,
   410→                now_monotonic,
   411→                last_activity,
   412→                stall_seconds,
   413→            )
   414→            if stalled:
   415→                stalled = True
   416→                with state.lock:
   417→                    state.runner_note = (
   418→                        f"stall recovery triggered after {stall_seconds}s "
   419→                        "with stable output state"
   420→                    )
   421→                recovered_from_stall = _output_file_has_json_payload(ctx.output_file)
   422→                _terminate_process(process)
   423→                break
   424→        deps.sleep_fn(min(interval, 1.0))
   425→
   426→    if process.poll() is None:
   427→        _terminate_process(process)
   428→    stdout_thread.join(timeout=2)
   429→    stderr_thread.join(timeout=2)
   430→    state.stop_event.set()
   431→    writer_thread.join(timeout=2)
   432→    _write_live_snapshot(state, ctx)
   433→
   434→    return _ExecutionResult(
   435→        code=int(process.returncode or 0),
   436→        stdout_text="".join(state.stdout_chunks),
   437→        stderr_text="".join(state.stderr_chunks),
   438→        timed_out=timed_out,
   439→        stalled=stalled,
   440→        recovered_from_stall=recovered_from_stall,
   441→    )
   442→
   443→
   444→def _run_via_subprocess(
   445→    cmd: list[str],
   446→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
   447→    state: _RunnerState,
   448→    ctx: _AttemptContext,
   449→    interval: float,
   450→) -> _ExecutionResult:
   451→    """Execute batch via subprocess.run (compatibility path for tests)."""
   452→    writer_thread = _start_live_writer(state, ctx, interval)
   453→    try:
   454→        result = deps.subprocess_run(
   455→            cmd,
   456→            capture_output=True,
   457→            text=True,
   458→            timeout=deps.timeout_seconds,
   459→        )
   460→    except deps.timeout_error as exc:
   461→        state.stop_event.set()
   462→        writer_thread.join(timeout=2)
   463→        ctx.log_sections.append(
   464→            f"{ctx.header}\n\nTIMEOUT after {deps.timeout_seconds}s\n{exc}\n"
   465→        )
   466→        ctx.safe_write_text_fn(ctx.log_file, "\n\n".join(ctx.log_sections))
   467→        return _ExecutionResult(code=124, stdout_text="", stderr_text="", early_return=124)
   468→    except OSError as exc:
   469→        state.stop_event.set()
   470→        writer_thread.join(timeout=2)
   471→        ctx.log_sections.append(f"{ctx.header}\n\nRUNNER ERROR:\n{exc}\n")
   472→        ctx.safe_write_text_fn(ctx.log_file, "\n\n".join(ctx.log_sections))
   473→        return _ExecutionResult(code=127, stdout_text="", stderr_text="", early_return=127)
   474→    except (RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:  # pragma: no cover - defensive boundary
   475→        state.stop_event.set()
   476→        writer_thread.join(timeout=2)
   477→        ctx.log_sections.append(f"{ctx.header}\n\nUNEXPECTED RUNNER ERROR:\n{exc}\n")
   478→        ctx.safe_write_text_fn(ctx.log_file, "\n\n".join(ctx.log_sections))
   479→        return _ExecutionResult(code=1, stdout_text="", stderr_text="", early_return=1)
   480→    finally:
   481→        state.stop_event.set()
   482→        writer_thread.join(timeout=2)
   483→
   484→    return _ExecutionResult(
   485→        code=int(result.returncode),

   487→        stderr_text=result.stderr or "",
```

```
   663→def write_packet_snapshot(
   664→    packet: dict,
   665→    *,
   666→    stamp: str,
   667→    review_packet_dir: Path,
   668→    blind_path: Path,
   669→    safe_write_text_fn,
   670→) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
   671→    """Persist immutable and blind packet snapshots for runner workflows."""
   672→    review_packet_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   673→    packet_path = review_packet_dir / f"holistic_packet_{stamp}.json"
   674→    safe_write_text_fn(packet_path, json.dumps(packet, indent=2) + "\n")
   675→    blind_packet = _build_blind_packet(packet)
   676→    safe_write_text_fn(blind_path, json.dumps(blind_packet, indent=2) + "\n")
   677→    return packet_path, blind_path
   678→
   679→
   680→def _build_blind_packet(packet: dict) -> dict:
   681→    """Return a blind-review packet with score anchoring metadata removed."""
   682→    blind = deepcopy(packet)
   683→    for key in _BLIND_PACKET_DROP_KEYS:
   684→        blind.pop(key, None)
   685→
   686→    config = blind.get("config")
   687→    if isinstance(config, dict):
   688→        sanitized = _sanitize_blind_config(config)
   689→        if sanitized:
   690→            blind["config"] = sanitized
   691→        else:
   692→            blind.pop("config", None)
   693→    return blind
   694→
   695→
   696→def build_blind_packet(packet: dict) -> dict:
   697→    """Public wrapper for blind packet sanitization."""
   698→    return _build_blind_packet(packet)
   699→
   700→
   701→def _sanitize_blind_config(config: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   702→    """Drop score/target hints from config while preserving unrelated options."""
   703→    sanitized: dict[str, Any] = {}
   704→    for key, value in config.items():
   705→        lowered = key.strip().lower()
   706→        if not lowered:
   707→            continue
   708→        if lowered in _BLIND_CONFIG_SCORE_HINT_KEYS:
   709→            continue
   710→        if "target" in lowered:
   711→            continue
   712→        if lowered.endswith("_score"):
   713→            continue
   714→        sanitized[key] = value
   715→    return sanitized

   718→def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str | None:
   719→    """Compute sha256 hex digest for path contents (or None on read failure)."""
   720→    try:
   721→        data = path.read_bytes()
   722→    except OSError:
   723→        return None
   724→    return sha256(data).hexdigest()

   782→    run_dir = run_root / stamp

   814→
   815→def _emit_progress(
   816→    progress_fn,
   817→    batch_index: int,
   818→    event: str,
   819→    code: int | None = None,
   820→    *,
   821→    details: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   822→) -> Exception | None:
   823→    """Forward a progress event and return callback exceptions to caller."""
   824→    if not callable(progress_fn):
   825→        return None
   826→    payload = dict(details or {})
   827→    progress_event = BatchProgressEvent(
   828→        batch_index=batch_index,
   829→        event=event,
   830→        code=code,
   831→        details=payload,
   832→    )
   833→    try:
   834→        progress_fn(progress_event)
   835→        return None
   836→    except TypeError as exc:
   837→        if not _looks_like_progress_signature_mismatch(exc):
   838→            return RuntimeError(
   839→                f"progress callback failed for event={event} batch={batch_index}: {exc}"
   840→            )
   841→        try:
   842→            progress_fn(batch_index, event, code, **payload)
   843→            return None
   844→        except Exception as legacy_exc:
   845→            return RuntimeError(
   846→                f"progress callback failed for event={event} batch={batch_index}: {legacy_exc}"
   847→            )
   848→    except Exception as exc:
   849→        return RuntimeError(
   850→            f"progress callback failed for event={event} batch={batch_index}: {exc}"
   851→        )
   852→
   853→
   854→def _record_execution_error(
   855→    *,
   856→    error_log_fn,
   857→    failures: set[int],
   858→    idx: int,
   859→    exc: Exception,
   860→) -> None:
   861→    """Record an execution/progress error through shared failure plumbing."""
   862→    if callable(error_log_fn):
   863→        try:
   864→            error_log_fn(idx, exc)
   865→        except (OSError, TypeError, ValueError):
   866→            pass  # error-logging callback failed; don't crash the batch
   867→    failures.add(idx)
   868→
   869→
   870→def execute_batches(
   871→    *,
   872→    tasks: dict[int, object],
   873→    run_parallel: bool,
   874→    progress_fn=None,
   875→    error_log_fn=None,
   876→    max_parallel_workers: int | None = None,
   877→    heartbeat_seconds: float | None = 15.0,
   878→    clock_fn=time.monotonic,
   879→) -> list[int]:
   880→    """Run indexed tasks and return failed index list.
   881→
   882→    Each value in *tasks* is a zero-arg callable returning an int exit code.
   883→    All domain knowledge (files, prompts, etc.) is pre-bound by the caller.
   884→    """
   885→    indexes = sorted(tasks)
   886→    if run_parallel:
   887→        return _execute_parallel(
   888→            tasks=tasks,
   889→            indexes=indexes,
   890→            progress_fn=progress_fn,
   891→            error_log_fn=error_log_fn,
   892→            max_parallel_workers=max_parallel_workers,
   893→            heartbeat_seconds=heartbeat_seconds,
   894→            clock_fn=clock_fn,
   895→        )
   896→    return _execute_serial(
   897→        tasks=tasks,
   898→        indexes=indexes,
   899→        progress_fn=progress_fn,
   900→        error_log_fn=error_log_fn,
   901→        clock_fn=clock_fn,
   902→    )
   903→
   904→
   905→def _execute_serial(*, tasks, indexes, progress_fn, error_log_fn, clock_fn) -> list[int]:
   906→    """Run tasks one at a time — no threads, no closures."""
   907→    failures: set[int] = set()
   908→    for idx in indexes:
   909→        t0 = float(clock_fn())
   910→        start_error = _emit_progress(
   911→            progress_fn, idx, "start", None, details={"max_workers": 1}
   912→        )
   913→        if start_error is not None:
   914→            _record_execution_error(
   915→                error_log_fn=error_log_fn,
   916→                failures=failures,
   917→                idx=idx,
   918→                exc=start_error,
   919→            )
   920→        try:
   921→            code = tasks[idx]()
   922→        except Exception as exc:
   923→            _record_execution_error(
   924→                error_log_fn=error_log_fn,
   925→                failures=failures,
   926→                idx=idx,
   927→                exc=exc,
   928→            )
   929→            code = 1
   930→        if code != 0:
   931→            failures.add(idx)
   932→        done_error = _emit_progress(
   933→            progress_fn, idx, "done", code,
   934→            details={"elapsed_seconds": int(max(0.0, clock_fn() - t0))},
   935→        )
   936→        if done_error is not None:
   937→            _record_execution_error(
   938→                error_log_fn=error_log_fn,
   939→                failures=failures,
   940→                idx=idx,
   941→                exc=done_error,
   942→            )
   943→    return sorted(failures)
   944→
   945→
   946→def _execute_parallel(
   947→    *, tasks, indexes, progress_fn, error_log_fn,
   948→    max_parallel_workers, heartbeat_seconds, clock_fn,
   949→) -> list[int]:
   950→    """Run tasks in a thread pool with optional heartbeat monitoring.
   951→
   952→    Two closures (_run_one, _on_complete) share mutable executor state
   953→    (started_at, failures) under a lock — the natural pattern for thread-pool
   954→    work.
   955→    """
   956→    requested = (
   957→        int(max_parallel_workers)
   958→        if isinstance(max_parallel_workers, int) and max_parallel_workers > 0
   959→        else 8
   960→    )
   961→    max_workers = max(1, min(len(indexes), requested))
   962→    heartbeat = (
   963→        float(heartbeat_seconds)
   964→        if isinstance(heartbeat_seconds, int | float) and heartbeat_seconds > 0
   965→        else None
   966→    )
   967→
   968→    failures: set[int] = set()
   969→    progress_failures: set[int] = set()
   970→    started_at: dict[int, float] = {}
   971→    lock = threading.Lock()
   972→
   973→    def _on_progress_error(idx: int, err: Exception) -> None:
   974→        with lock:
   975→            progress_failures.add(idx)
   976→        if callable(error_log_fn):
   977→            try:
   978→                error_log_fn(idx, err)
   979→            except (OSError, TypeError, ValueError):
   980→                pass  # error-logging callback failed; don't crash the batch
   981→
   982→    def _run_one(idx: int) -> int:
   983→        with lock:
   984→            started_at[idx] = float(clock_fn())
   985→        progress_error = _emit_progress(
   986→            progress_fn, idx, "start", None,
   987→            details={"max_workers": max_workers},
   988→        )
   989→        if progress_error is not None:
   990→            _on_progress_error(idx, progress_error)
   991→        return tasks[idx]()
   992→
   993→    def _on_complete(future) -> None:
   994→        idx = futures[future]
   995→        with lock:
   996→            t0 = started_at.get(idx, float(clock_fn()))
   997→        elapsed = int(max(0.0, clock_fn() - t0))
   998→        try:
   999→            code = future.result()
```
